       Devoted to bringing you great taste and a smile to your face
                             致力帶給您幸福的美味
Nissin Foods is committed to providing the highest quality products to customers. We do so by
continuously conducting research and product development meticulously, and always attaching
great importance to innovation, food quality and safety. We have also kept investing in people and
improving our production process and capabilities, so as to strengthen our overall competitiveness
and ensure sustainable growth.

Following our Group Philosophy - to be the Earth Food Creator, we contribute to society by
gratifying people with the pleasure of eating, and to the world by creating food, good food.


日清食品致力為顧客提供最優質的產品。為此，我們持續和嚴謹地進行研究及產品開發，對創
新、食品質量及安全高度重視，始終如一。與此同時，我們繼續投放資源在人力資源上，不斷
改善生產流程和提升生產力，從而提高整體競爭力以及確保可持續發展。

貫徹本集團「Earth Food Creator」的理念，我們通過創造優質美味的食物，為人類帶來飲食的
樂趣，藉此貢獻社會和世界。




          “Nissin Foodium” for you to discover more wonders and
                        surprises from Nissin Foods
           「日清食品體驗館」— 發掘更多樂趣和驚喜
To further enhance customer experience and connect with consumers, Nissin Foods has launched
its first-ever membership programme in Hong Kong and Mainland China. “Nissin Foodium”, a
WeChat mini program, offers a host of exclusive benefits, exciting rewards and interactive games
for members to discover and enjoy, in addition to providing the latest brand and product news. In
Mainland China, it is also an e-commerce platform. “Nissin Foodium” connects us to our
end-consumers, allowing direct communication for us to better understand consumer
preferences, behaviours and changing consumption trends.

We, at Nissin Foods, never stop learning and innovating.


為進一步提升顧客體驗以及連繫消費者，日清食品於中國內地及香港推出首個會員計劃。我們
通過「日清食品體驗館」微信小程式，除了為會員帶來最新品牌及產品資訊外，還讓會員發掘
及體驗一系列會員尊享禮遇、精彩獎賞及互動遊戲。於中國內地，它更是一個電子商務平台。
「日清食品體驗館」將我們與消費者聯繫起來，直接交流，從而讓我們更了解消費者的喜好、
行為，以及不斷變化的消費趨勢。

在日清食品，我們學而不倦，不斷創新。

株主の皆様へ
日清食品有限公司（以下「当社」）董事会（以下「董事会」）を代表し、当社および子会社（以下「グループ」）の 2022 年 12 月 31
日終了年度の年次報告書をお届けします。


増収増益を達成
2022 年はコロナ最終年で、上半期はパンデミック対応に注力しました。また、原材料価格の高騰やサプライチェーン寸断
による香港の物流・保管コストの急騰が見られ、包括的なコスト削減や価格高騰対策を実施しました。当社グループは当
社創業者       安藤百福氏の企業理念である『食為聖職（食に関わる仕事は神聖な職業である）』のもと、年間を通じて生産に
支障なく商品を供給し、消費者の皆様の食に貢献しました。


当グループは、昨年を上回る業績を達成しました。株主に帰属する利益は、前年同期比 4.2％増の 3 億 1,651 万香港ドルと
なりました。香港における国内外向け即席麺の旺盛な需要と、香港及び中国における価格調整により、原材料や物流・保
管コストの高騰を吸収し増益になりました。グループ売上高は、前年同期比約 4.1％増加し、40 億 2,498 万香港ドルとな
りました。中国事業は、2022 年後半に消費マインドが弱まったものの、販売数量の増収傾向を維持しました。一方、香港
事業では国内外からの旺盛な需要により、即席麺の販売数量が年間を通じて増加しました。また、点心分野のマーケット
シェア 1 位となった冷凍食品は、堅調な需要により年間売上を拡大しました。




                                                                                 ANNUAL REPOR T 2022   二零二二年年報   09
     CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT 董事長報告


     プレミアム化
     過去 10 年間で中国の一人当たりの所得が増加し、消費の質の向上が人 々の生活の中に浸透しました。2021 年、当社はカ
     ップヌードル ( 合味道 ) をリニューアルし、より滑らかな麺、スープの品質を向上、新たな容器に更新しました。


     また香港事業では、2020 年に 100％北海道産小麦粉使用の出前一丁を香港で発売しました。4 つのフレーバーを展開し、よ
     り多くの消費者の皆様に様 々な味わいをお届けしました。更に 2022 年に中国でも 100％北海道産小麦粉使用の出前一丁の
     発売を開始し、引き続き今後もプレミアム商品の発売を進めていきます。


     事業への投資
     日清食品のブランドロイヤリティを高めるため、会員制プログラム Nissin Foodium を 2022 年 7 月に香港で、8 月に中国で
     開始しました。カップヌードル ( 合味道 ) の裏蓋の QR コードをスマートフォンで読み取ると WeChat ミニプログラムのポイ
     ントを獲得、様 々なアイテムやノベルティと交換することができます。このデジタルマーケティングプロモーションは、
     消費者一人ひとりの嗜好を把握し、強いてはその声を新商品開発に反映させるができます。更に QR コードを印刷した新し
     い形状の蓋は、お湯を注いだ後、従来の蓋より簡単かつ確実に容器に密着させることができ、消費者の利便性を向上させ
     ています。


     グループの拡大について、2022 年がグループ事業にとって極めて重要な年になりました。2022 年 11 月、即席麺事業の拡
     大とプレミアム化の流れに沿 った当社の発展のため、1993 年の設立以来、中国地方政府との合弁会社である珠海永南を当
     社の間接完全子会社としました。


     サステナビリティ
     創業者          安藤百福氏の『食創為世（食を創ることで社会に奉仕する）』の企業理念のもと、サステナビリティ経営に取り組
     んでいます。責任ある企業・市民として長期的な成長を目指し、グループが着実に成長しながら地域社会の持続的発展に
     貢献すると信じています。


     環境経営
     浙江工場では太陽光発電パネルを追加設置し発電能力を拡大しました。更に 2021 年 12 月に稼働した珠海日清包装工場では
     新たに太陽光発電システムを導入しました。2023 年には、珠海永南に新たな太陽光発電システムを導入予定です。今後も
     グループ全体で太陽光発電システムを継続し、再生可能エネルギーを活用した事業展開を行 っていく予定です。


     環境経営の観点から、カップヌードル ( 合味道 ) の新型蓋は従来品よりも PET（ポリエチレンテレフタレート）の使用量を削
     減することに成功しました。


     人事管理
     当社グループは、将来の持続的な成長のため、採用、入社、その後のステップに沿 った人材育成のため投資を継続してい
     ます。新入社員を対象に、ビジョン、文化、即席麺、開発方法、マーケティング、販売方法などに関する知識を社員講師
     が講義し、香港と中国の両方で社員の意識向上と業務ノウハウの習得を図る研修を年 10 回実施しています。また、医療保
     険など従業員に対する福利厚生制度を強化するとともに、従業員への健康診断プログラム、インフルエンザワクチンの接
     種を毎年実施しています。




10   N I S S I N F O O D S CO M PA N Y L I M I T E D   日清食品有限公司
コミュニティー 投資
地域社会への貢献のため、2020 年に「日清チャリティーファンド」を設立しました。香港中文大学では、食品科学者育成
のための奨学金制度を開始し、2022 年度には 5 名の学部生と 2 名の博士課程の学生が授与しました。また、香港教育大学
では、将来の食育指導者の奨学金制度を開始し、2022 年に 4 名の学生が授与しました。当社グループは、今後も優秀な学
生への奨学金給付を継続していきます。


2023 年の見通し
香港および中国の直近のコロナ感染状況は落ち着いています。パーム油を中心とした原材料価格が安定化し、海上運賃を
中心とした物流コストが正常化してきており、当社はこの様な状況から恩恵を受けると見込んでいます。中国では中央政
府が経済、特に消費喚起を促しており、香港特別行政区政府はすでに景気刺激策と観光客の新たな呼び込み策を開始し、
中国や海外から多くの観光客が香港に戻 ってくることを期待しています。


中国事業ではカップヌードル（合味道）をはじめとする即席麺の販売数量を大幅に拡大し、特に中国西部・北部の主要顧
客や地元商店向けの卸店網を拡大することに注力します。また、中国で発生したパンデミック時には、人流が鈍化しまし
た。今後は、小売店や交通機関など、人流が多いところで販促や広告宣伝を増していきます。非即席麺事業では、中国の
卸店事業で日本製菓子の輸入販売の拡大に努めます。


香港事業では 100％北海道産小麦粉使用の出前一丁 の販売拡大に注力します。卸店事業ではパンデミック時に売上が大幅
に減少した観光・飲食業向けのミネラルウォーターの需要を取り込み、冷凍事業は高級冷凍食品を中心に海外需要を取り
込んでいきます。


董事長の使命と決意
2023 年はポストパンデミックの始まりとなり、当社グループの真価が問われる年です。私たちは中国や香港でカップヌー
ドル ( 合味道 ) 、出前一丁 、その他プレミアム商品をより多く浸透させることに最大限の努力を払い、販売数量の高い成長
軌道を取り戻し、非即席麺事業ではより多くの利益を上げていきます。


私はグループの事業運営と組織を効率的に発展させながら事業の成果を最大化し、当社の株主や投資家を含むすべてのス
テークホルダーに長期的な価値を創造することを目指したいと考えています。そして、当社グループは、地域社会の持続
的な成長に貢献していきます。


このような目標を念頭に置き、私は、創業者   安藤百福氏の『食創為世（食を創 って社会に奉仕する）』という企業理念の
もと、美味しく安全で求めやすい食品の生産と流通を通じて、私たちの生活を豊かにしていきます。




Kiyotaka Ando                       安藤清隆
Chairman                            董事長

     Risk Management and Internal Control                                            風險管理及內部監控
     The Group has formulated and adopted the risk management policy in              本集團已制定及採納風險管理政策以為識別、評
     providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing significant         估及管理主要風險提供指導。主要風險管理程序
     risks. The main features of the risk management processes comprise 5            包括五個核心流程：(a) 風險確定；(b) 風險評估及
     core stages: (a) risk identification, (b) risk assessment and prioritisation,   其風險優先次序；(c) 風險應對；(d) 風險監控；及
     (c) risk response, (d) risk monitoring and (e) risk reporting.                  (e) 風險匯報。

     The appropriate risk mitigation plans are determined based on the               本集團根據以下風險應對措施釐定適當的風險減
     following risk responses:                                                       緩計劃：

     –       Acceptance: Risks are considered immaterial and acceptable              －   接受：根據本集團之風險接受程度，風險
             based on Group’s risk appetite, and therefore no action is                  被視為並不重大及可接受，故毋須採取任
             considered necessary.                                                       何行動。




50   N I S S I N F O O D S CO M PA N Y L I M I T E D   日清食品有限公司
–     Reduction: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and actions           －   降低：風險被視為不能忽視，並採取監控
      such as controls have to be taken to reduce the impact and                  措施等行動以減少影響及風險承受程度至
      vulnerability to an acceptable level.                                       可接受水平。

–     Sharing: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and the Group           －   分擔：風險被視為不能忽視，本集團本身
      itself cannot effectively reduce the risks to an acceptably low             無法自行將風險有效降至可接受的水平，
      level, therefore a portion of the risks has to be transferred to or         故須透過保險及外判等方式將部分風險轉
      shared with other parties by insurance, outsourcing, etc.                   移至或與其他各方共同分擔。

–     Avoidance: Risks are so significant that there is no measure,           －   防範：風險被視為相當重大，本集團沒有
      both internally and externally, to reduce the risk to an                    足夠及有效的內部監管系統及外界措施可
      acceptable level or involve unreasonably high costs to reduce               將風險降至可接受水平或需不合理及高昂
      the risk to an acceptable level. Therefore, activities giving rise to       之成本將風險降至可接受水平。因此，須
      risks should be avoided.                                                    避開產生有關風險之活動。

The Group has adopted the COSO ERM Framework, the “Top-Down”                  本集團已採納COSO的風險管理框架（「由上而下」
approach, in establishing its risk management framework, which                方式）由董事會、審核委員會及風險管理專責小
consists of the Board, the Audit Committee, the Risk Management               組及各有關部門組成的風險管理及內部監控架
Taskforce and various departments within the Group.                           構。

and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness. The           檢討其成效。董事會釐定達致本集團業務目標所

     (i)     Food Safety Risk                                                   (i)    食物安全風險
     The Group’s success depends on its branding and reputation and             本集團的成功依賴於其品牌聲譽，以及消費者的

     and cause a loss of consumers’ trust in its products. The profitability    令消費者對產品失去信心，從而可能影響本集團
     of the Group may be impacted as a result.                                  的盈利能力。

     As a food manufacturer, the Group places high emphasis on food             作為產品製造商，本集團非常重視食物安全及質
     safety and quality management to ensure the health and safety of its       量管理，以確保其客人的健康及安全。因此，本
     customers. Therefore, the food production plants of the Group are          集團的食物生產工廠均已取得 ISO 22000（一項
     all certified under ISO 22000, an international standard for quality       有關品質管理系統的國際標準）的所有認證。此
     management systems. In addition, the Fujian Plant, Shunde Plant,           外，福建廠房、順德廠房、香港日清廠房及香港

Public Health                                                               公眾健康

     SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                           股份獎勵計劃
     A share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme” or the “Scheme”)              本公司於 2016 年 3 月 7 日（「採納日期」）採納股
     was adopted by the Company on 7 March 2016 (the “Adoption                    份獎勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」或「該計劃」）。
     Date”).

     Purpose                                                                      目的
     The purpose of the Share Award Scheme is to recognise the                    股份獎勵計劃的目的為認可部分經選定僱員（包
     contribution of certain selected employees (including but not limited        括但不限於本集團任何成員公司的董事、行政人
     to directors, executives, officers and other employees, whether full-        員、高級職員及其他僱員（全職或兼職））的貢
     time or part-time, of any members of our Group) and to provide the           獻，並向僱員給予獎勵，以留住彼等為本集團的
     employees with incentives in order to retain them for the continual          持續營運及發展效力。
     operation and development of the Group.

     Eligible participants                                                        合資格參與者
     Participants include any employee, including without limitation, the         參與者包括任何僱員，包括但不限於本集團任何
     director, executive, officer and other employees, whether full-time or       成員公司不時的董事、行政人員、高級職員及其
     part-time, of any member of the Group from time to time.                     他僱員（全職或兼職）。

     Term                                                                         期限
     Subject to any early termination as may be determined by the Board           除非受董事會根據該計劃規則提出任何提前終止
     pursuant to the Scheme Rules, the Share Award Scheme shall be valid          所限，股份獎勵計劃於採納日期起計 10 年期間有
     and effective from the Adoption Date for a term of 10 years. The             效及生效。截至本年報日期，該計劃的剩餘年限
     remaining life of Scheme is approximately 2 years 11 months as at the        約為 2 年 11 個月。
     date of this annual report.

     Grant and acceptance                                                         授出及接受
     Subject to the terms of the Scheme and the Listing Rules, the Board          除非受該計劃的條款及上市規則的規限，董事會
     may, from time to time, grant awards to any eligible participant who         可不時向其認為已或將為本集團作出貢獻之任何
     the Board considers to have contributed or will contribute to the            合資格參與者授出獎勵。
     Group.

     To satisfy the awards, the Company may transfer to the trustee the           為落實獎勵，本公司應向受託人劃撥所需資金，
     necessary funds and instruct the trustee to acquire Shares through on-       並指示受託人按當時市價透過場內交易收購股
     market transactions at the prevailing market price.                          份。

     Vesting                                                                      歸屬
     The awarded Shares and related income shall be vested in the selected        根據該計劃的條款和以下條件，獎勵股份及相關
     employee at no cost to such selected employee on the vesting date            收入應於歸屬日期以零成本歸屬於有關經選定僱
     subject to the terms of the Scheme and the following conditions:             員：

     (a)     fulfilment of such vesting conditions (if any) as specified by the   (a)   達成本公司向經選定僱員發出的要約函中
             Board in the offer letter issued by the Company to the selected            董事會指定的有關歸屬條件（如有），且令
             employee up to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board;                   董事會合理滿意；

     (b)     the selected employee remaining under the employment of              (b)   經選定僱員截至歸屬日期（包括當日）一直
             any member of the Group up to and including the vesting date               受僱於本集團成員公司，且該日並無相關
             and no notice of termination of employment of such selected                經選定僱員的離職通知；及
             employee is subsisting as at such date; and

     (c)     the execution by the selected employee of the relevant               (c)   經選定僱員簽署相關文件，在規定的期限
             documents to effect the transfer of the awarded Shares and the             內將獎勵股份及相關收入由受託人轉至經
             related income from the trustee to the selected employee within            選定僱員。
             the stipulated period.

and the PRC laws. During the year ended 31 December 2022 and                      年度及截至本年報日期，本公司已遵守於香港及
up to the date of this annual report, the Company has complied                    中國有重大影響之相關法律及法規。具體而言，
with the relevant laws and regulations that have significant impact               作為飲食產品製造商，本集團營運受香港及中國
in Hong Kong and the PRC. In particular, as a food and beverage                   之食品安全及環保法律及法規規管。於回顧年
manufacturer, the Group’s operations are regulated by the food safety             度，本集團並無任何嚴重違反有關法律及法規之
and environmental protection laws and regulations in Hong Kong and                情況。
the PRC. During the year under review, the Group did not have any

PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION                                                獲准許之彌償條文
Pursuant to the Articles of Association, every directors shall be entitled   根據組織章程細則並在公司條例准許之情況下，
to the indemnity out of the assets of the Company against any liability      每名董事均有權就其涉及本公司之任何民事或刑
incurred by him/her in relation to the Company in defending any              事訴訟進行抗辯而招致之任何負債，從本公司資
proceedings, whether civil or criminal, to the extent permitted by the       產中獲得彌償。有關獲准許之彌償條文已於截至
Companies Ordinance. Such permitted indemnity provision was in               2022 年 12 月 31 日止年度生效，並於本年報日期
force during the year ended 31 December 2022 and remains in force            一直生效。
at the date of this annual report.

     CONNECTED TRANSACTION                                                       關連交易
     During the year ended 31 December 2022, the Company entered into            截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日止年度，本公司根據上市
     a connected transaction under the Listing Rules. We set out below           規則訂立關連交易。根據上市規則第 14A 章的規
     details of the connected transaction for our Group, in compliance           定，我們於下文載列本集團關連交易的詳情。
     with the requirements of Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules.

     On 10 November 2022, Nissin Foods (China) Holding Co., Ltd. (“Nissin        於 2022 年 11 月 10 日， 本 公 司 全 資 附 屬 公 司 日
     Foods China”), the wholly-owned subsidiary of the Company, and              清食品（中國）投資有限公司（「日清食品中國」）
     Zhuhai Western Development Co entered into the equity transfer              與珠海西部發展有限公司訂立股權轉讓協議，
     agreement, pursuant to which Nissin Foods China acquired 29.55%             據此，日清食品中國透過公開出售於廣東聯合
     equity interest (the “Acquisition”) in Zhuhai Golden Coast Winner           產權交易中心（「廣東聯合產權交易中心」）收購
     Food Products (“Zhuhai Winner”) through the public sale on                  珠海市金海岸永南食品有限公司（「珠海永南」）
     Guangdong United Asset and Equity Exchange (“GUAEX”) at a cash              29.55% 股權（「收購事項」），現金代價為人民幣
     consideration of RMB352,440,500 (the “Consideration”). Zhuhai               352,440,500 元（「代價」）。珠海西部發展有限公
     Western Development Co is a connected person of the Company at              司因作為珠海永南之主要股東而為本公司附屬公
     the subsidiary level by virtue of being a substantial shareholder of        司層面之關連人士。代價指日清食品中國在公開
     Zhuhai Winner. The Consideration represents the successful bid price        出售於廣東聯合產權交易中心所提交的成功中標
     submitted by Nissin Foods China at the public sale on GUAEX, which          價，亦為就出售於廣東聯合產權交易中心所提供
     was also the minimum bid price offered for sale on GUAEX. The Board         的最低競標價。董事認為收購事項可讓本公司鞏
     is of the view that the Acquisition allows the Company to consolidate       固其對珠海永南的控制權，而珠海永南為本集團
     its control over Zhuhai Winner which is an integral part of the Group’s     於中國內地營運不可或缺的一部分，並可更靈活
     operation in Mainland China, and allows greater flexibility to deploy       地部署本集團於中國內地的產能，以應對市場需
     the Group’s production capabilities in Mainland China to respond to         求及不斷變化的商業環境，從而提升本集團之整
     market demand and the changing business environment, which will             體收入及盈利能力。收購事項於 2022 年 11 月 28
     in turn increase the overall income and profitability of the Group.         日完成。自完成當日起，珠海永南成為本公司之

years ended 31 December 2019, 2020 and 2021, the total assets,                   近三個財政年度的財務報表，於最近三個財政年

less than 10% under the percentage ratios for each of the latest                 率相比本集團均低於 10%。因此，根據上市規
three financial years. Accordingly, MCMS will be regarded as an                  則第 14A.09 條，香港捷菱將被視為本公司的非
insignificant subsidiary of the Company under 14A.09 of the Listing              重大附屬公司，而三菱商事不再是本公司關連人
Rules, and Mitsubishi Corporation is no longer a connected person                士。故此，過往年度與三菱商事集團簽訂的四份
of the Company. Therefore, the transactions contemplated under the               總協議（即香港三菱總採購協議、香港三菱總供
four master agreements with Mitsubishi Group (i.e., MCHK Master                  應協議、三菱商事總供應協議及三菱食品總採購

in the previous year are exempt from annual review and are not                   披露。倘香港捷菱不再是本公司的非重大附屬公
disclosed in the annual report for the year ended 31 December 2022.              司，本公司應就本集團與三菱商事的交易重新遵

OPINION                                                                   意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Nissin Foods     本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第 91

     independence, and to communicate with them all relationships            合理地被認為會影響我們獨立性的所有關係和其
     and other matters that may reasonably be thought to bear on our         他事項，以及在適用的情況下，採取行動消除威
     independence, and where applicable, actions taken to eliminate          脅或應用防範措施。
     threats or safeguards applied.


     From the matters communicated with those charged with governance,       從與治理層溝通的事項中，我們確定哪些事項對
     we determine those matters that were of most significance in the        本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要，因而構成關
     audit of the consolidated financial statements of the current period    鍵審計事項。我們在核數師報告中描述這些事
     and are therefore the key audit matter. We describe these matters       項，除非法律法規不允許公開披露這些事項，或
     in our auditor’s report unless law or regulation precludes public       在極端罕見的情況下，如果合理預期在我們報告

     we determine that a matter should not be communicated in our            益，我們決定不應在報告中溝通該事項。

         The Group has applied the amendments for the first                       本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂

         HKFRS 9 Financial Instruments                                           香港財務報告準則第9號財務工具
         The amendment clarifies that for the purpose of assessing                該修訂本澄清，為評估在「10%」標
         whether modification of terms of original financial liability            準下對原始財務負債條款的修改是否
         constitutes substantial modification under the “10 per                   構成實質性修改，借款人僅包括在借
         cent” test, a borrower includes only fees paid or received               款人與貸款人之間已支付或收取的費
         between the borrower and the lender, including fees paid                 用，包括由借款人或貸款人代表對方
         or received by either the borrower or the lender on the                  支付或接收的費用。
         other’s behalf.


         In accordance with the transitional provisions, the Group                根據過渡性條文，本集團應用於首次
         applies the amendment to financial liabilities that are                  應 用 日 期（2022 年 1 月 1 日）已 修 改
         modified or exchanged as at the date of initial application,             或交換的金融負債之修訂本。
         1 January 2022.


         HKFRS 16 Leases                                                         香港財務報告準則第 16 號租賃

         reimbursement relating to leasehold improvements by the                  租人為租賃物業裝修而作出補償說
         lessor in order to remove any potential confusion.                       明，以消除任何潛在之混淆。


         HKAS 41 Agriculture                                                     香港會計準則第 41 號農業
         The amendment ensures consistency with the requirements                  該修訂本刪除了香港會計準則第 41
         in HKFRS 13 Fair Value Measurement by removing the                       號第 22 段中關於使用現值技術計量
         requirement in paragraph 22 of HKAS 41 to exclude                        生物資產的公平值時不包括稅收現金
         taxation cash flows when measuring the fair value of a                   流量之要求，從而確保與香港財務報

              effective (continued)                                                   財務報告準則（續）
              Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice                                 香港會計準則第 1 號及香港財務報告準
              Statement 2 Disclosure of Accounting Policies                           則實務報告第 2 號（修訂本）會計政策
                                                                                      披露
              HKAS 1 is amended to replace all instances of the term                  香港會計準則第 1 號修訂以「重大會計政策
              “significant accounting policies” with “material accounting             資料」取代「主要會計政策」一詞的所有情
              policy information”. Accounting policy information is material          況。倘連同實體財務報表內其他資料一併
              if, when considered together with other information included in         考慮，會計政策資料可以合理預期會影響
              an entity’s financial statements, it can reasonably be expected         通用財務報表的主要使用者根據該等財務
              to influence decisions that the primary users of general purpose        報表所作出的決定，則該會計政策資料屬
              financial statements make on the basis of those financial               重大。
              statements.


              The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information          該 等 修 訂 亦 澄 清， 即 使 涉 及 款 項 並 不 重
              may be material because of the nature of the related                    大，但基於相關交易性質、其他事項或情
              transactions, other events or conditions, even if the amounts           況，會計政策資料仍可屬重大。然而，並
              are immaterial. However, not all accounting policy information          非所有與重大交易、其他事項或情況有關
              relating to material transactions, other events or conditions           的會計政策資料本身即屬重大。倘一間實
              is itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose immaterial         體選擇披露非重大會計政策資料，有關資
              accounting policy information, such information must not                料不得掩蓋重大會計政策資料。
              obscure material accounting policy information.


              HKFRS Practice Statement 2 Making Materiality Judgements                香港財務報告準則實務報告第 2 號作出重大
              (the “Practice Statement”) is also amended to illustrate how an         性判斷（「實務報告」）亦經修訂，以說明一
              entity applies the “four-step materiality process” to accounting        間實體如何將「四步法評估重大性流程」應
              policy disclosures and to judge whether information about               用於會計政策披露及判斷有關一項會計政
              an accounting policy is material to its financial statements.           策的資料對其財務報表是否屬重大。實務
              Guidance and examples are added to the Practice Statement.              報告已增加指導意見及實例。


              The application of the amendments is not expected to have               應用該等修訂預期不會對本集團財務狀況
              significant impact on the financial position or performance             或表現產生重大影響，但可能影響本集團
              of the Group but may affect the disclosures of the Group’s              主要會計政策之披露。
              significant accounting policies.

     policy. Developing accounting estimates involves the use of

3.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                         3.   綜合財務報表編製基準及主
     STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                  要會計政策（續）
     3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                          3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準（續）
         statements (continued)
          The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 除下文會計政策所詳述若干於各報告
          on the historical cost basis except for certain financial                期末按公平值計量之金融工具外，綜
          instruments that are measured at fair values at the end                  合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製。
          of each reporting period, as explained in the accounting
          policies set out below.


          The directors of the Company have, at the time of                        本公司董事於批准綜合財務報表時合
          approving the consolidated financial statements, a                       理預期，本集團有充足資源於可預見
          reasonable expectation that the Group has adequate                       未來持續經營。因此，彼等繼續採用
          resources to continue in operational existence for the                   持續經營基準會計處理方法編製綜合
          foreseeable future. Thus, they continue to adopt the                     財務報表。
          going concern basis of accounting in preparing the
          consolidated financial statements.


          Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本通常基於交換貨品及服務時
          consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  所提供代價之公平值。


          Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                公平值為於計量日期市場參與者於有
          asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction          秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負
          between market participants at the measurement date,                     債須支付之價格，而不論該價格是否
          regardless of whether that price is directly observable                  直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估
          or estimated using another valuation technique. In                       計。倘市場參與者於計量日期對資產
          estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the                或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債之特
          Group takes into account the characteristics of the                      點，則本集團於估計資產或負債之公
          asset or liability if market participants would take                     平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財
          those characteristics into account when pricing the                      務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途之公
          asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value                   平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財
          for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these                      務報告準則第 2 號以股份為基礎付款
          consolidated financial statements is determined on such                  範圍內之以股份為基礎付款交易、根
          a basis, except for share-based payment transactions that                據香港財務報告準則第 16 號入賬的
          are within the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment,                     租賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若
          leasing transactions that are accounted for in accordance                地方但並非公平值之計量，譬如香港

                       A business is an integrated set of activities and assets               業務為一整套活動及資產，包括輸入
                       which includes an input and a substantive process                      參數及共同為創造輸出能力作出重大

                       the related processes or they significantly contribute to the          貢獻且視為獨特或罕見或在毋須付出

                       unique or scarce or cannot be replaced without significant             或導致持續產生輸出的能力延誤。
                       cost, effort, or delay in the ability to continue producing
                       outputs.

                       the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange                   本一般於產生時在損益確認。
                       for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are

                       of any non-controlling interests in the acquiree and the               象權益的公平值（如有）的總和，則
                       fair value of the acquirer’s previously held interest in the           超出部分即時於損益確認為議價購買
                       acquiree (if any), the excess is recognised immediately in             收益。
                       profit or loss as a bargain purchase gain.


                       Non-controlling interests that are present ownership                   屬現時擁有權益且於清盤時讓持有人
                       interests and entitle their holders to a proportionate                 有權按比例分佔相關附屬公司資產淨
                       share of the relevant subsidiary’s net assets in the event             值的非控股權益，初步按非控股權益
                       of liquidation are initially measured at the non-controlling           分佔收購對象可識別資產淨值的已確
                       interests’ proportionate share of the recognised amounts               認金額比例或公平值計量。
                       of the acquiree’s identifiable net assets or at fair value.


                      Goodwill                                                                商譽
                       Goodwill arising on an acquisition of a business is carried            收購業務產生之商譽按收購業務日期
                       at cost as established at the date of acquisition of the               之成本減累計減值虧損（如有）列賬。
                       business less accumulated impairment losses, if any.


                       For the purposes of impairment testing, goodwill is                    就減值測試而言，商譽會分配予預期
                       allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or             會從合併獲得協同效益之本集團各
                       group of cash-generating units) that is expected to benefit            個現金產生單位（或現金產生單位組
                       from the synergies of the combination, which represent                 別），即商譽就內部管理而言之最低
                       the lowest level at which the goodwill is monitored for                監控水平，且不會大於營運分部。
                       internal management purposes and not larger than an
                       operating segment.

          Revenue from contracts with customers                                   來自客戶合約收入
          The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                 本集團於履行履約義務時（或就此）
          obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or            確認收入，即與特定履約義務相關的
          services underlying the particular performance obligation               貨品或服務的「控制權」轉移予客戶
          is transferred to the customer.                                         時確認收入。


          A performance obligation represents a good or service (or               履約責任指一個明確貨品或一項明確
          a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of          服務（或一批明確貨品或服務）或一
          distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.             系列大致相同的明確貨品或服務。


          Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised              倘符合下列其中一項標準，商品或服
          over time by reference to the progress towards complete                 務的控制權在一段時間內轉移，而收
          satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one              入確認會按一段時間內已完成相關履
          of the following criteria is met:                                       約責任的進度進行：


          •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                 •     於本集團履約時，客戶同時取
                the benefits provided by the Group’s performance                        得並耗用本集團履約所提供的
                as the Group performs;                                                  利益；


          •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an                    •     本集團的履約產生或提升一項
                asset that the customer controls as the Group                           資產，而該項資產於本集團履
                performs; or                                                            約時由客戶控制；或


          •     the Group’s performance does not create an asset                  •     本集團的履約並未產生對本集
                with an alternative use to the Group and the                            團有替代用途的資產，且本集
                Group has an enforceable right to payment for                           團對迄今已完成履約的付款具
                performance completed to date.                                          有可強制執行的權利。


          Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when                否則，收入會在當客戶獲得明確貨物
          the customer obtains control of the distinct good or                    或服務的控制權時在某一時點確認。
          service.

                       Variable consideration                                                 可變代價
                       For contracts that contain variable consideration, such                就訂有可變代價（如銷售回贈）之合
                       as sales rebate, the Group estimates the amount of                     約 而 言， 本 集 團 以 (a) 預 期 價 值 法；
                       consideration to which it will be entitled using either (a)            或 (b) 最有可能獲得之金額估計其可
                       the expected value method or (b) the most likely amount,               獲得之代價金額，取於那方法較佳預
                       depending on which method better predicts the amount                   測本集團可獲得之代價金額。
                       of consideration to which the Group will be entitled.


                       The estimated amount of variable consideration is                      可變代價之估計金額乃包含於交易價
                       included in the transaction price only to the extent that              格 內， 惟 僅 當 可 變 代 價 之 相 關 不 確
                       it is highly probable that such an inclusion will not result           定性在其後變得確定，致使有關估計
                       in a significant revenue reversal in the future when the               金額很大可能不會導致於將來出現重
                       uncertainty associated with the variable consideration is              大收益撥回，其方可包含於交易價格
                       subsequently resolved.                                                 內。

              3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                             3.2 主要會計政策（續）
                      Leases                                                                租賃
                      Definition of a lease                                                 租賃的定義
                       A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys          倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
                       the right to control the use of an identified asset for a            內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
                       period of time in exchange for consideration.                        合約是租賃或包含租賃。


                       For contracts entered into or modified on or after                   就於首次應用日期或之後或因業務合
                       the date of initial application or arising from business             併而訂立或修改的合約而言，本集團
                       combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is               根據香港財務報告準則第 16 號的定
                       or contains a lease based on the definition under HKFRS              義於初始或修改日期（如適用）評估
                       16 at inception or modification date, as appropriate.                該合約是否為租賃或包含租賃。除非
                       Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and            合約的條款及條件其後出現變動，否
                       conditions of the contract are subsequently changed. As              則有關合約將不予重新評估。作為簡
                       a practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics           易實務處理方法，當本集團合理預期
                       are accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group                    對綜合財務報表的影響將不會與組合
                       reasonably expects that the effects on the consolidated              內的個別租賃顯著不同時，具有類似
                       financial statements would not differ materially from                特徵的租賃按組合基準入賬。
                       individual leases within the portfolio.


                       The Group as a lessee                                                本集團作為承租人
                       Allocation of consideration to components of a contract              將代價分配至合約的組成部分
                       For a contract that contains a lease component and one               就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項
                       or more additional lease or non-lease components, the                額外租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而
                       Group allocates the consideration in the contract to each            言，本集團依照租賃組成部分的相對
                       lease component on the basis of the relative stand-alone             獨立價格及非租賃組成部分的總獨立
                       price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-                價格，將合約中的代價分配至各組賃
                       alone price of the non-lease components.                             組成部分。


                       Non-lease components are separated from lease                        非租賃部份獨立於租賃部份，並應用
                       component and are accounted for by applying other                    其他適用準則處理。
                       applicable standards.


                       Short-term leases                                                    短期租賃
                       The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                   本集團對自開始日期起計租期為 12
                       exemption to leases of staff quarters that have a lease              個月或以下且不包含購買選擇權的員
                       term of 12 months or less from the commencement date                 工宿舍租賃應用短期租賃的確認豁
                       and do not contain a purchase option. Lease payments on              免。短期租賃的租賃付款在租賃期內
                       short-term leases are recognised as expense on a straight-           採用直線基準或其他系統性基準確認
                       line basis or another systematic basis over the lease term.          為支出。

                liability;

                commencement date, less any lease incentives                                    租賃付款，減任何已收租賃優
                received;                                                                       惠；


          •     any indirect costs incurred by the Group; and                             •     本集團產生的任何直接成本；

                the terms and conditions of the lease.                                          本估計。

          adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                            何重新計量作出調整。


          Right-of-use assets in which Group is reasonably certain                        本集團於租期屆滿時合理確定獲得相

          Otherwise, right-of-use assets are depreciated on a                             期（以較短者為準）內按直線法基準

          life and the lease term.

     STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)             要會計政策（續）
     3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                        3.2 主要會計政策（續）
          Borrowing costs                                                     借貸成本
          Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,           直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格
          construction or production of qualifying assets, which are          資產（指必須經一段長時間處理以作
          assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time           其預定用途或銷售的資產）的借貸成
          to get ready for their intended use or sale, are added to           本，加入該等資產的成本內，直至資
          the cost of those assets until such time as the assets are          產大致上備妥供其預定用途或銷售為
          substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                 止。


          All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss          所有其他借貸成本於其產生期內損益
          in the period in which they are incurred.                           中確認。


          Government grants                                                   政府補貼
          Government grants are not recognised until there is                 政府補貼須於能合理確定本集團將遵
          reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the            守附帶條件及將會收到補貼時方會確
          conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be            認。
          received.


          Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on               政府補貼於本集團確認補貼所擬定補
          a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group              償之有關成本為開支之期間有系統地
          recognises as expenses the related costs for which                  於損益內確認。具體而言，主要條件
          the grants are intended to compensate. Specifically,                為本集團須購買、建設或以其他方式
          government grants whose primary condition is that the               收購非流動資產之政府補貼於綜合財
          Group should purchase, construct or otherwise acquire               務狀況表內確認為遞延收入，並按有
          non-current assets are recognised as deferred income                系統及合理的基準於有關資產之可使
          in the consolidated statement of financial position and             用年期轉撥至損益。
          transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational
          basis over the useful lives of the related assets.


          Government grants related to income that are receivable             作為已產生開支或虧損之補償或用作
          as compensation for expenses or losses already incurred             為本集團提供即時財務支持而無未來
          or for the purpose of giving immediate financial support            相關成本之與收入相關應收政府補貼
          to the Group with no future related costs are recognised            於應收期間在損益內確認。該等補貼
          in profit or loss in the period in which they become                呈列為「其他收入」。
          receivable. Such grants are presented under “other
          income”.

                       plans are recognised as an expense when employees have                乃於僱員提供服務而有權享有供款時

                       term employee benefits are recognised as an expense.

                       (such as wages and salaries, annual leave and sick leave)             利（如工資和薪酬、年假和病假）確

                       Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax                       所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅
                       currently payable and deferred tax.                                    項之總和。


                       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit                   即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計
                       for the year. Taxable profit differs from profit before                算。應課稅溢利與「除稅前溢利」不
                       taxation because of income or expense that are taxable                 同，乃由於其他年度之應課稅或可扣
                       or deductible in other years and items that are never                  稅收入或開支以及從毋須課稅或不能
                       taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax           扣稅之項目。本集團之即期稅項負債
                       is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                乃根據於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已
                       substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.              頒佈之稅率計算。


                       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                    遞延稅項乃就綜合財務報表內資產及
                       between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities                 負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所用
                       in the consolidated financial statements and the                       相應稅基之暫時性差額確認。遞延稅
                       corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                     項負債一般就所有應課稅暫時性差額
                       taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                 確認。遞延稅項資產一般會於可能出
                       recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred             現應課稅溢利以抵銷此等可扣稅之暫
                       tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                 時性差額時就所有可扣稅之暫時性差
                       temporary differences to the extent that it is probable                額確認。倘暫時性差額源自初步確認
                       that taxable profits will be available against which those             （除業務合併外）不影響應課稅溢利
                       deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                 或會計溢利之交易之資產及負債，則
                       deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the          有關遞延稅項資產及負債不會予以確
                       temporary difference arises from the initial recognition               認。此外，倘於初步確認商譽後產生
                       (other than in a business combination) of assets and                   暫時性差額，遞延稅項負債將不會予
                       liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable          以遞延。
                       profit nor the accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax
                       liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference
                       arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.


                       Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable                    遞延稅項負債乃就於附屬公司及聯營
                       temporary differences associated with investments in                   公司投資相關之應課稅暫時性差額
                       subsidiaries and an associate, except where the Group is               確認，惟本集團可控制有關暫時性差
                       able to control the reversal of the temporary difference               額撥回且於可見將來可能不會撥回除
                       and it is probable that the temporary difference will not              外。僅於有足夠應課稅溢利可用於抵
                       reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets                 銷暫時性差額之抵免且預期暫時性差
                       arising from deductible temporary differences associated               額於可見將來撥回時，方會確認該等
                       with such investments are only recognised to the extent                投資相關之可扣稅暫時性差額所產生
                       that it is probable that there will be sufficient taxable              之遞延稅項資產。
                       profits against which to utilise the benefits of the

          indication exists, the recoverable amount of the relevant                           可收回金額以釐定減值虧損（如有）
          asset is estimated in order to determine the extent of the                          之程度。
          impairment loss, if any.


          T h e re c o v e r a b l e a m o u n t o f p ro p e r t y, p l a n t a n d          物業、廠房及設備、使用權資產及無
          equipment, right-of-use assets and intangible assets are                            形資產的可收回金額乃個別估計。倘
          estimated individually. When it is not possible to estimate                         不大可能估計個別可收回金額，則本
          the recoverable amount individually, the Group estimates                            集團估計資產所屬現金產生單位之可
          the recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit to                               收回金額。
          which the asset belongs.


          In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment,                                   於測試現金產生單位之減值時，當合
          corporate assets are allocated to the relevant cash-                                理而持續之分配基準能夠確定，公司
          generating unit when a reasonable and consistent basis                              資產分配予相關現金產生單位，否則
          of allocation can be established, or otherwise they are                             將分配至能確定合理而持續之分配基
          allocated to the smallest group of cash generating units                            準的最小現金產生單位組合內。可收
          for which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis can                          回金額是按公司資產所屬的現金產生
          be established. The recoverable amount is determined                                單位或現金產生單位組合釐定，並與
          for the cash-generating unit or group of cash-generating                            相關現金產生單位或現金產生單位組
          units to which the corporate asset belongs, and is                                  合的賬面值進行比較。
          compared with the carrying amount of the relevant cash-
          generating unit or group of cash-generating units.


          Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs                           可收回金額為公平值減出售成本與使
          of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use,                            用價值之較高者。評估使用價值時，
          the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their                             估計未來現金流量採用稅前折現率折
          present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects                           現至其現值，該折現率反映當前市場
          current market assessments of the time value of money                               所評估之貨幣時間價值及資產（或現
          and the risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating                           金產生單位）之特定風險，而估計未
          unit) for which the estimates of future cash flows have                             來現金流量尚未作出調整。
          not been adjusted.

                       The amount recognised as a provision is the best estimate             確認為撥備之金額乃於報告期末對解
                       of the consideration required to settle the present                   決當前責任所需代價的最佳估計，並

                       into account the risks and uncertainties surrounding                  因素。倘撥備乃按估計用以償付當前
                       the obligation. When a provision is measured using the                責任所需現金流量計算，其賬面值為
                       cash flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its            該等現金流量之現值（倘金錢時間價
                       carrying amount is the present value of those cash flows              值的影響為重大）。
                       (where the effect of the time value of money is material).


                      Contingent liabilities                                                 或然負債
                       A contingent liability is a present obligation arising                或然負債指因過往事件引致之現時責
                       from past events but is not recognised because it is                  任（但其因需要流出具有經濟利益之
                       not probable that an outflow of resources embodying                   資源以履行責任之機會不大或責任金
                       economic benefits will be required to settle the obligation           額不能足夠可靠地計量而不予確認）
                                                                                                            。
                       or the amount of the obligation cannot be measured with
                       sufficient reliability.


                       Where the Group is jointly and severally liable for an                如本集團對須共同及各別承擔某項責
                       obligation, the part of the obligation that is expected to            任，該項責任中預期由另一方承擔的
                       be met by other parties is treated as a contingent liability          部分則以或然負債處理，並不須綜合
                       and it is not recognised in the consolidated financial                財務報表中予以確認。
                       statements.


                       The Group assesses continually to determine whether an                本集團持續評估或然負債以釐定帶有
                       outflow of resources embodying economic benefits has                  經濟利益的資源流出是否已成為可
                       become probable. If it becomes probable that an outflow               能。倘有需要就一項先前作為或然負
                       of future economic benefits will be required for an item              債處理之項目付出未來經濟利益，則
                       previously dealt with as a contingent liability, a provision          於可能出現變動的報告期之綜合財務
                       is recognised in the consolidated financial statements in             報表內確認撥備（除非出現罕見情況
                       the reporting period in which the change in probability               而未能作出可靠估算）。
                       occurs, except in the extremely rare circumstances where
                       no reliable estimate can be made.

                 If the credit risk on the credit-impaired financial                   實際利率予以確認。倘信貸減

                 by applying the effective interest rate to the gross                  值，於釐定資產不再出現信貸
                 carrying amount of the financial asset from the                       減值後，自報告期開始起利息
                 beginning of the reporting period following the                       收入乃對金融資產賬面總值應
                 determination that the asset is no longer credit-                     用實際利率予以確認。
                 impaired.


          (ii)   Financial assets at FVTPL                                      (ii)   按公平值計入損益之金融資產
                 Financial assets that do not meet the criteria for                    並不符合條件按攤銷成本或按

              STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                       要會計政策（續）
              3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                                  3.2 主要會計政策（續）
                      Financial instruments (continued)                                          金融工具（續）
                      Financial assets (continued)                                               金融資產（續）
                       Impairment of financial assets                                            金融資產減值
                       The Group performs impairment assessment under                            本集團根據香港財務報告準則第 9 號
                       expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                    須 予 減 值 的 金 融 資 產， 按 預 期 信 貸
                       (including trade receivables, other receivables, loan                     虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進行減
                       receivable, amounts due from ultimate holding company                     值（包括貿易應收賬款、其他應收款
                       and fellow subsidiaries, bond receivables, time deposits                  項、應收貸款、應收最終控股公司及
                       with maturity over three months, time deposits with                       同系附屬公司款項、應收債券、三個
                       maturity over one year and bank balances) which are                       月以上定期存款、一年以上定期存款
                       subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS 9. The                       及銀行結餘）的預期信貸虧損確認虧
                       amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to                        損撥備。預期信貸虧損金額於各報告
                       reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition.                 日期更新，以反映自初始確認以來的
                                                                                                 信貸風險變動。


                       Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                     全期預期信貸虧損指相關工具於預計
                       all possible default events over the expected life of the                 全期內所有可能違約事件所產生的預
                       relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                      期 信 貸 虧 損。 相 反，12 個 月 預 期 信
                       ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                      貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指
                       expected to result from default events that are possible                  預計於報告日期後 12 個月內可能發
                       within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments                    生的違約事件產生的部分全期預期信
                       are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                      貸虧損。評估乃根據本集團過往信貸
                       experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the                 虧損經驗，並就債務人特定因素、一
                       debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                    般經濟狀況以及對於報告日期之當時
                       of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                   狀況及未來狀況預測的評估作調整。
                       well as the forecast of future conditions.


                       The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                        本集團一直確認貿易應收賬款及應收
                       receivables and amounts due from fellow subsidiaries                      同系附屬公司款項（屬貿易性質）之
                       which are in trade nature. The ECL on these assets are                    全期預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期
                       assessed individually for debtors with significant balances               信貸虧損乃就具有重大結餘的債務人
                       and collectively using a provision matrix with appropriate                進行個別評估及使用合適分組的撥備
                       groupings. The amounts due from subsidiaries which are                    矩陣進行整體評估。應收附屬公司款
                       in trade nature are assessed individually.                                項（屬貿易性質）乃個別評估。


                       For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                    就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量虧
                       allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been                    損撥備等於 12 個月預期信貸虧損，
                       a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
                       in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                      上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧
                       assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                   損。評估是否應確認全期預期信貸虧
                       is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk of            損乃根據自初始確認以來發生違約的
                       a default occurring since initial recognition.                            可能性或風險顯著上升。

          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                           預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                  率、違約損失率（即存在違約
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                      時的損失程度）及違約風險的
                the exposure at default. The assessment of the                        函數。違約概率及違約損失率
                probability of default and loss given default is based                乃基於根據前瞻性資料和歷史
                on historical data and forward-looking information.                   數據評估。預期信貸虧損估計
                Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                            反映公正及機率加權金額，此
                probability-weighted amount that is determined                        乃按相關違約發生風險為加權
                with the respective risks of default occurring as                     而釐定。本集團採用撥備矩陣
                the weights. The Group uses a practical expedient                     估計貿易應收款項的預期信貸
                in estimating ECL on trade receivables using a                        虧損，並考慮過往信貸虧損經
                provision matrix taking into consideration historical                 驗，並就不過多投入資金或精
                credit loss experience, adjusted for forward looking                  力即可獲得的前瞻性資料而作
                information that is available without undue cost or                   調整。
                effort.


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                      一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                      集團根據合約應收的所有合約
                in accordance with the contract and the cash flows                    現金流量與本集團預計收取的
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                      現金流量之間的差額，並按初
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                     始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。
                recognition.


                Lifetime ECL for certain trade receivables are                        若干貿易應收款項的全期預期
                considered on a collective basis taking into                          信貸虧損按逾期資料及相關信
                consideration past due information and relevant                       貸資料（如前瞻宏觀經濟資料）
                credit information such as f o r w a rd l o o k i n g                 等綜合基準考量。
                macroeconomic information.

                      Financial assets (continued)                                           金融資產（續）
                       Impairment of financial assets (continued)                            金融資產減值（續）
                       (v)     Measurement and recognition of ECL (continued)                (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                                                                                                   （續）
                               For collective assessment, the Group takes into                     就綜合評估而言，本集團於分
                               consideration the following characteristics when                    組時計及以下特徵：
                               formulating the grouping:

                               •        Past-due status;                                           •     逾期狀況；

                               •        Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                  •     債 務 人 的 性 質、 規 模 及
                                                                                                         行業；及

                               •        External credit ratings where available.                   •     外部信貸評級（如有）。

                               The grouping is regularly reviewed by management                    管理層會定期審閱該分類，以
                               to ensure the constituents of each group continue                   確保各組別的組成部分繼續分
                               to share similar credit risk characteristics.                       擔相似的信貸風險特徵。

                               Interest income is calculated based on the gross                    利息收入按金融資產的賬面總
                               carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                   值 計 算， 倘 金 融 資 產 信 貸 減
                               financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case                   值，則利息收入按金融資產的
                               interest income is calculated based on amortised                    攤銷成本計算。
                               cost of the financial asset.

                               The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss                     本集團通過調整所有金融工具
                               in profit or loss for all financial instruments by                  的賬面值於損益確認其減值收
                               adjusting their carrying amount, with the exception                 益或虧損，惟透過虧損撥備賬
                               of trade receivables where the corresponding                        目確認相應調整的貿易應收賬
                               adjustment is recognised through a loss allowance                   款則除外。
                               account.

                               Derecognition of financial assets                                   終止確認金融資產
                               The Group derecognises a financial asset only                       僅當資產現金流量之合約權利
                               when the contractual rights to the cash flows from                  屆滿時，或其將金融資產以及
                               the asset expire, or when it transfers the financial                資產擁有權之絕大部分風險及
                               asset and substantially all the risks and rewards of                回報轉移予另一實體時，本集
                               ownership of the asset to another entity.                           團方會終止確認該金融資產。

                               amortised cost, the difference between the asset’s                  金融資產時，資產賬面值與已
                               carrying amount and the sum of the consideration                    收及應收代價間之差額於損益

                               loss.

     STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                 要會計政策（續）
     3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                            3.2 主要會計政策（續）
          Financial instruments (continued)                                       金融工具（續）
          Financial liabilities and equity                                        金融負債及權益
          Classification as debt or equity                                        分類為債務或權益
          Debt and equity instruments are classified as either                    債務及權益工具乃根據合約安排之內
          financial liabilities or as equity in accordance with the               容與金融負債及權益工具之定義分類
          substance of the contractual arrangements and the                       為金融負債或權益。
          definitions of a financial liability and an equity instrument.


          Equity instruments                                                      權益工具
          An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a                   權益工具為證明經扣除其所有負債後
          residual interest in the assets of an entity after deducting            之實體資產剩餘權益之任何合約。由
          all of its liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the                本公司發行之權益工具乃按所收取所
          Company are recognised at the proceeds received, net of                 得款項（扣除直接發行成本）確認。
          direct issue costs.


          Financial liabilities at amortised cost                                 按攤銷成本列賬之金融負債
          Financial liabilities (including trade payables, other                  金融負債（包括貿易應付款項、其他
          payables and accruals, and amounts due to ultimate                      應付款項及應計費用及應付最終控股
          holding company and fellow subsidiaries) are subsequently               公司及同系附屬公司款項）其後將使
          measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest                用實際利息法按攤銷成本計量。
          method.


          Derecognition of financial liabilities                                  終止確認金融負債
          The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and                  本集團於或僅於本集團之責任獲解
          only when, the Group’s obligations are discharged,                      除、取消或屆滿時，終止確認金融負
          cancelled or have expired. The difference between the                   債。所終止確認金融負債之賬面值與
          carrying amount of the financial liability derecognised and             應付代價之差額於損益內確認。
          the consideration and payable is recognised in profit or
          loss.


          Changes in the basis for determining the contractual cash               利率基準改革導致合同現金流量的釐
          flows as a result of interest rate benchmark reform                     定基礎變更
          For changes in the basis for determining the contractual                對於採用攤銷成本計量的金融資產或
          cash flows of a financial asset or financial liability to               金融負債的合同現金流量的釐定基礎
          which the amortised cost measurement applies as a result                因利率基準改革而導致的變更，本集
          of interest rate benchmark reform, the Group applies                    團採用實務變通方法通過更新實際利
          the practical expedient to account for these changes                    率來核算這些變化利率的變動，通常
          by updating the effective interest rate, such change in                 對相關金融資產或金融負債的賬面價
          effective interest rate normally has no significant effect              值沒有重大影響。
          on the carrying amount of the relevant financial asset or
          financial liability.

              differ from these estimates.

              ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised            會計估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間，

              Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an                       釐定商譽有否減值須估計獲分配商譽之現

              value less costs of disposal. The value in use calculation requires        期現金產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）將

              downward revision of future cash flows or upward revision of

              decision maker, i.e. the managing director of the Company.

    Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime, the first               根 據 兩 級 制 利 得 稅 率 制 度， 合 資 格 集 團
    HK$2,000,000 of profits of the qualifying group entity will be         實 體 的 首 2 百 萬 港 元 溢 利 將 按 8.25% 的
    taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2,000,000 will be taxed           稅率徵稅，而超過 2 百萬港元的溢利將按
    at 16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for the         16.5% 的稅率徵稅。不符合兩級制利得稅
    two-tiered profits tax rates regime will continue to be taxed at a     率制度資格之集團實體之溢利將繼續按稅
    flat rate of 16.5%.                                                    率 16.5% 繳納稅項。


    Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax (the “EIT            根據中國企業所得稅法（「企業所得稅法」）
    Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT Law, the tax            及企業所得稅法實施條例，中國內地附屬
    rate of Mainland China subsidiaries is 25% for both years.             公司兩個年度之稅率均為 25%。


    The EIT Law requires withholding tax to be levied on distribution      企業所得稅法規定，中國內地實體向香港
    of profits earned by Mainland China entities for profits               居民企業（為已收股息實益擁有人）就其所

      35. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEME (continued)                                                   35. 退休福利計劃（續）
              Defined benefit plan                                                                   定額福利計劃
              Pursuant to the Employment Ordinance, Chapter 57, the Group                            根據僱傭條例第 57 章，本集團有責任在退
              has the obligation to pay Long Service Payment (“LSP”) to                              休後基於以下公式向符合條件的香港僱員
              qualifying employees in Hong Kong upon retirement, subject                             支付長期服務金（「長期服務金」），最低僱
              to a minimum of 5 years employment period, based on the                                傭期為 5 年：
              following formula:


              Last monthly wages (before termination of employment) × 2/3                            最近一個月工資（僱傭終止前）× 2/3 × 服
              × Years of service                                                                     務年限

      37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                       37. 資本風險管理
              The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the                本集團之資本管理旨在確保本集團旗下實
              Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                     體將能持續經營，同時透過經營產生之溢
              maximising the return to shareholders through the generation                利為利益相關方爭取最高回報。本集團之
              of profits from its operations. The Group’s overall strategy                整體策略較過往年度維持不變。
              remains unchanged from prior year.


              The capital structure of the Group consists of cash and cash                本集團資本結構包括現金及現金等價物及
              equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the Company,               本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及
              comprising issued share capital and reserves.                               儲備）。


              The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an             本公司董事按年度基準審閱資本結構。作
              annual basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the            為此審閱之一環，董事考慮資本成本及與
              cost of capital and the risks associated with its capital. Based on         資本相關之風險。本集團將根據董事之意
              recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance its                見透過支付股息及發行新股來平衡其整體
              overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and              資本結構。
              new share issues.

           nature, other receivables, loan receivable, amount due                   款、應收最終控股公司款項、銀行結
           from ultimate holding company, bank balances, time                       餘、三個月以上定期存款及一年以上

           deposits with maturity over one year. The Group does                     品或其他信貸增級以填補與金融資產
           not hold any collateral or other credit enhancements to                  有關的信貸風險，惟與應收貸款有關
           cover its credit risks associated with its financial assets,             的信貸風險已降低，因為其以債務人

                       equivalents deemed adequate by the management to                              等價物水平，為本集團之營運撥付資
                       finance the Group’s operations and mitigate the effects of                    金，並減輕現金流量波動之影響。
                       fluctuations in cash flows.
